# Ares tags

Sources: the public [Ares documentation](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/). Ares is closed source, so every row rests on that documentation alone. A tick (✔) marks a tag this engine already reads. Sizes, file lists and dependencies are estimates from file names; effects are unverified until tested with the real game. Rows are grouped by the system they extend, with the same group names as [`phobos-tags.md`](phobos-tags.md) where one fits; the source link names the Ares page a row came from.

| `Abductor` | rules: WeaponType | A hit pulls the target into the attacker's passenger hold instead of damaging it, falling back to normal damage if the hold is full or the target cannot be taken. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/chronoprisons.html) | `code/weapon.cpp`, `code/bullet.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | large | passenger hold, `SizeLimit` |
| `Abductor.Temporal` | rules: WeaponType | Delays abduction until the target has been temporally erased, then places it inside the attacker; if that fails the target is erased as usual. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/chronoprisons.html) | `code/weapon.cpp`, `code/temporal.cpp` | medium | `Abductor`, warhead `Temporal=yes` |
| `Abductor.Anim` | rules: WeaponType | Animation spawned where a unit is abducted from. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/chronoprisons.html) | `code/weapon.cpp`, `code/anim.cpp` | small | `Abductor` |
| `Abductor.ChangeOwner` | rules: WeaponType | Gives the abducted unit to the abductor's house unless it is immune to psionics. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/chronoprisons.html) | `code/weapon.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | `Abductor` |

| `SW.Range` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Most superweapons having a ranged effect can take a float or two integers. | `SW.Group` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Distinguish multiple super weapons of the same type. Defaults to 0. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/general.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | small | - |
| `SW.AffectsHouse` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Which houses items are affected by this super weapon. You can combine multiple values by comma. Values: none, owner, allies, team, enemies, all. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/general.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | medium | - |
| `SW.AffectsTarget` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Which items are allowed to be affected by this super weapon. You can combine multiple values by comma. | `SW.InitialReady` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Whether the first super weapon of this type will be ready to fire immediately after the super weapon becomes available. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/general.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | small | - |
| `SW.VirtualCharge` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Once the super weapon becomes available, it will charge continuously, even if the the owning player loses all buildings that provide it. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/general.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | medium | - |
| `SW.PostDependent` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Super weapon invoked right after this one fires; in the stock game only the Chronosphere uses it, to invoke the Chronowarp. `PreClick`, `PostClick` and `PreDependent` are fixed and no longer have any effect. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/hardcoded.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | small | - |
| `Light.Enabled` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Whether the lighting gets respected or not. Currently only the primary super weapons support lighting changes. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/lighting.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | medium | - |
| `Light.Ambient` | rules: SuperWeaponType | The brightness of the environment. Too high values will cause a slow-down. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/lighting.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | medium | `Light.Enabled` |

| `SW.RangeMaximum` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Distance in cells the target coordinates may be away from a player’s building providing this super weapon. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/range.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | small | - |
| `SW.RangeMinimum` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Distance in cells the target coordinates must be away from a player’s building providing this super weapon. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/range.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | small | - |

| `SW.UseAITargeting` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Whether AI targeting will decide the target used when firing this super weapon. If yes, clicking the cameo will not allow manual target selection and instead fire the super weapon at an automatically determined location. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/targeting.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | medium | - |
| `SW.AutoFire` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Sets whether this super weapon will be launched automatically once ready even for human players. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/targeting.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | medium | - |
| `SW.ManualFire` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Sets whether this super weapon can be manually fired by the owning player. If set to no, the player will not be able to get a selection cursor for this super weapon when clicking the cameo. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/targeting.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | medium | `SW.AutoFire` |
| `SW.FireIntoShroud` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Whether or not this super weapon is allowed to fire into an unexplored area of the map. Default is yes. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/targeting.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | small | - |
| `SW.RequiresTarget` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Which items this super weapon can fire at. Hovering above an allowed item will show the Cursor, otherwise the player gets the NoCursor and it is not possible to launch the super weapon. Values: none, land, water, empty, infantry, units, buildings. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/targeting.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | medium | - |
| `SW.RequiresHouse` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Which house’s items this super weapon can fire at. Values: none, owner, allies, team, enemies, all. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/targeting.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | medium | - |
| `SW.AITargeting` | rules: SuperWeaponType | How the AI, or a player with `SW.UseAITargeting` or `SW.AutoFire`, picks the target: None, Nuke, LightningStorm, PsychicDominator, GeneticMutator, IronCurtain, ForceShield, ParaDrop, DropPod, Offensive, MultiMissile, HunterSeeker, NoTarget, Stealth, Self, Base, EnemyBase, Attack, LowPower, LowPowerAttack or LightningRandom. The defaults of the other `SW.AI*` keys follow this value and the super weapon `Type`. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/targeting.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp`, `code/house.cpp` | large | - |

| `SW.AIRequiresHouse` | rules: SuperWeaponType | Specifies which houses will be considered eligible by AI players or human owned automatically fired super weapons. Values: owner, allies, team, enemies, others, all. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/superweapons/targeting.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/suprtype.cpp` | medium | `SW.AITargeting` |

| `Script action 65 (Auxiliary Power)` | script action | Permanently adds (cumulatively) the given power amount to the team owner's output; format 65,power. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/scripts.html#auxiliary-power-65) | `code/script.cpp`, `code/house.cpp` | small | - |
| `Script action 66 (Kill Drivers)` | script action | Kills drivers of all team units, respecting protection, and moves them to the Special country; format 66,0. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/new/scripts.html#kill-drivers-66) | `code/script.cpp`, `code/unit.cpp` | medium | `KillDriver`, `ProtectedDriver` |

## Bug fixes

| Tag | Section or type | Effect | Source | Files in `code/` | Size | Depends on |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Aircraft manual docking | Bug fix (rules: AircraftType) | Players can order aircraft to land on any structure in the AircraftType's `Dock` list, with enter or no-enter cursors over structures. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/aircraftdock.html) | `code/aircraft.cpp`, `code/building.cpp`, `code/mouse.cpp` | medium | `Dock` |
| AI super weapons ignore cloaked targets | Bug fix (rules: SuperWeaponType) | AI-fired Lightning Storm, Multi Missile, Psychic Dominator and Genetic Converter no longer pick cloaked objects as targets. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/aitargetingcloakedobjectswithmajorsuperweapons.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/house.cpp` | small | `Type=LightningStorm` |
| Animated cursors | Bug fix (interface) | Animates the Paradrop, Chronosphere, IronCurtain, Detonate and IvanBomb cursors, which have several frames but showed only the first. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/animatedcursors.html) | `code/mouse.cpp` | small | - |
| Bouncing animation without a warhead | Bug fix (art: AnimType) | A bouncing animation without a `Warhead` deals no damage instead of crashing. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/animdamage.html) | `code/anim.cpp` | small | `Warhead` |
| Battle Bunkers at red health | Bug fix (rules: BuildingType) | Infantry may still enter a Battle Bunker at red health; other occupiable structures still refuse. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/battlebunkerredhealth.html) | `code/building.cpp`, `code/infantry.cpp` | small | - |
| Behind animations and open-topped transports | Bug fix (art: AnimType) | Behind animations are no longer shown for passengers of `OpenTopped` vehicles. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/behindanimopentopped.html) | `code/anim.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | `OpenTopped` |
| Blocking enemy refineries and war factories | Bug fix (movement) | Enemy units can no longer stand on the passable cells of a refinery or war factory and block it. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/blockedrefinerywarfactory.html) | `code/cell.cpp`, `code/building.cpp` | medium | - |
| Burst abuse | Bug fix (rules: WeaponType) | A unit that loses or changes its target mid-burst keeps the burst counter instead of resetting it, so it cannot fire a fresh burst at once. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/burstabuse.html) | `code/techno.cpp` | small | `Burst` |
| Chaos gas and the stop command | Bug fix (rules: WarheadType) | Units under `Psychedelic=yes` gas ignore the stop command. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/chaosgasandstopcommand.html) | `code/techno.cpp` | small | `Psychedelic` |
| Checksum buffer overrun | Bug fix (network) | Fixes a write past class bounds in the desync checksum code. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/checksumimpl.html) | `code/crc.cpp` | small | - |
| Aborted chronoshift announcement | Bug fix (interface) | Cancelling the target selection of a Chronosphere no longer plays the "ready" EVA line. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/chronoshiftabort.html) | `code/super.cpp` | small | - |
| Chronoshifted hover jumpjets sink | Bug fix (rules: UnitType) | A jumpjet with `SpeedType=Hover` chronoshifted onto water sinks instead of taking off unharmed. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/chronoshiftwillsinkjumpjetunits.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/unit.cpp` | small | `SpeedType` |
| Construction yard capture crash | Bug fix (buildings) | Capturing a construction yard while its owner builds a structure no longer crashes. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/conyardcapture.html) | `code/building.cpp`, `code/house.cpp` | small | - |
| Distance overflow in dock search | Bug fix (movement) | Distances are capped at the largest value, so far structures are no longer preferred over near ones when a unit looks for a refinery, airport or depot. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/distanceoverflow.html) | `code/coord.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | - |
| Guard order on enemy harvesters | Bug fix (interface) | Players can no longer give the guard order to enemy harvesters. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/enemyharvesterguardmodeexploit.html) | `code/unit.cpp` | small | - |
| Elite firing voices fall back to the non-elite list | Bug fix (rules: TechnoType) | An empty `VoicePrimaryEliteWeaponAttack` or `VoiceSecondaryEliteWeaponAttack` list falls back to the non-elite weapon list instead of `VoiceAttack`. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/firingvoicesandveterancy.html) | `code/techno.cpp`, `code/techtype.cpp` | small | `VoicePrimaryWeaponAttack` |
| AI Force Shield only against fired weapons | Bug fix (superweapons) | The AI uses its Force Shield only against super weapons that actually fired, not ones whose launch was refused. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/forceshieldexploit.html) | `code/super.cpp`, `code/house.cpp` | small | - |
| Mutation animation memory leak | Bug fix (art: AnimType) | A `MakeInfantry` animation is deleted when the new infantry cannot be placed, instead of leaking memory each frame. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/frozenmutationmakeinfantryanimations.html) | `code/anim.cpp` | small | `MakeInfantry` |
| Guard mission does not chase aircraft | Bug fix (missions) | Units on Guard mission stay in place when an enemy aircraft leaves range; Area Guard is unchanged. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/guardapproachtarget.html) | `code/techno.cpp`, `code/mission.cpp` | small | - |
| Infantry deploy jam | Bug fix (rules: InfantryType) | Completes the state machine so `Deployer=yes` infantry with `UndeployDelay` of 0 or more no longer hang after deploying. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/infantrydeploy.html) | `code/infantry.cpp` | small | `Deployer`, `UndeployDelay` |
| Ivan bombs survive deploy, owner change and sale | Bug fix (weapons) | A bomb moves with a unit that deploys or a building that undeploys, survives an owner change, and detonates when its host is sold. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/ivanbombsdisappearing.html) | `code/techno.cpp`, `code/building.cpp` | medium | - |
| Crazy Ivan and Grinders | Bug fix (interface) | An allied Grinder shows the enter cursor to a bomb-planting infantry; an enemy Grinder shows the bomb cursor; force fire still plants a bomb on an allied one. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/ivanentergrinder.html) | `code/infantry.cpp`, `code/mouse.cpp` | small | - |
| Stale target after destruction | Bug fix (combat) | Units and structures drop a destroyed target correctly, which removes frequent aircraft crashes. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/lasttargetbug.html) | `code/techno.cpp`, `code/object.cpp` | small | - |
| Load screen text colours | Bug fix (interface) | Allies get `AlliedLoad` blue in campaigns, and Yuri gets `YuriLoad` (purple if unset) in multiplayer, instead of `SovietLoad` red. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/loadscreencolors.html) | `code/load.cpp` | small | `AlliedLoad`, `YuriLoad` |
| Freed mind-controlled buildings guard | Bug fix (rules: BuildingType) | A building freed from mind control, or released by its controller, is put on Guard instead of idling. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/mindcontrolbuildings.html) | `code/building.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | - |
| Veteran mark on naval cameos | Bug fix (interface) | Infiltrating a war factory marks only the cameos of vehicles that will start veteran from it, not those built at a naval yard. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/misleadingveterannavalcameos.html) | `code/sidebar.cpp`, `code/building.cpp` | small | - |
| Passive houses cannot be favourite enemy | Bug fix (rules: Country) | `MultiplayPassive=yes` countries are never picked as an AI player's favourite enemy. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/multiplaypassiveanger.html) | `code/house.cpp` | small | `MultiplayPassive` |
| NoSpawnAlt body under a temporal weapon | Bug fix (rules: UnitType) | A `NoSpawnAlt=yes` spawner keeps the voxel that matches its real spawn state while a temporal weapon freezes it. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/nospawnalttemporal.html) | `code/unit.cpp` | small | `NoSpawnAlt` |
| Occupiers promote at once | Bug fix (rules: InfantryType) | Infantry occupying a structure is promoted immediately and silently, without leaving the building first. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/occupierpromotion.html) | `code/infantry.cpp`, `code/building.cpp` | small | - |
| Open-topped transports jamming defences | Bug fix (rules: UnitType) | An open-topped vehicle full of fast-firing infantry no longer stops charging defensive structures from firing. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/opentoppedjambuildings.html) | `code/building.cpp`, `code/techno.cpp` | small | `OpenTopped` |
| Naval parasites and allied kills | Bug fix (rules: UnitType) | `Naval=yes` parasites no longer gain experience from grabbing and killing allied vessels. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/parasitesexperience.html) | `code/techno.cpp` | small | `Naval` |
| Parasite in an airborne unit | Bug fix (rules: UnitType) | A parasite inside a unit destroyed in flight appears at that height and falls, dying on impact; jumpjet parasites are not supported yet. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/parasitesinairborneunits.html) | `code/techno.cpp`, `code/aircraft.cpp` | small | - |
| Pip lookup corruption | Bug fix (rules: TechnoType) | `Pip` and `OccupyPip` no longer take wrong values when a section exists without them. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/pipdistortionbug.html) | `code/techtype.cpp` | small | `Pip`, `OccupyPip` |
| Prism support fixes | Bug fix (rules: BuildingType) | Maps that restate `[General]` no longer multiply `PrismSupportModifier` by 100; towers that are incapacitated stop supporting; towers under construction or being sold cannot support. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/prismsupportbugs.html) | `code/building.cpp`, `code/rules.cpp` | medium | `PrismSupportModifier`, `PrismForwarding.SupportModifier` |
| Minimising with a radial indicator | Bug fix (interface) | The game no longer crashes when minimised while a radial indicator is visible. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/radialindicatorcrash.html) | `code/display.cpp` | small | - |
| Crates after loading a save | Bug fix (saves) | Collected crates disappear correctly, and the code that removed all crates after loading is gone, so mission-critical crates survive a load. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/savegamecrates.html) | `code/cell.cpp`, `code/scenario.cpp` | small | - |
| Secret Lab boon weighting | Bug fix (rules: BuildingType) | Each Secret Lab picks its boon uniformly at random, independent of other labs. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/secretlabboonweighting.html) | `code/building.cpp` | small | - |
| Selling exploits | Bug fix (buildings) | A sell order cannot become a move order for a packing construction yard; crew is not ejected twice when a selling building is destroyed; the MCV is placed on the map or removed. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/sellingbuildingsexploits.html) | `code/building.cpp` | medium | - |
| Repair immunity at a shipyard | Bug fix (combat) | A unit being repaired at a shipyard takes damage from conventional weapons. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type1/shipyardrepairimmune.html) | `code/techno.cpp`, `code/building.cpp` | small | - |

| Pips loading | Bug fix (performance) | Loads `pips.shp` once instead of six times. | [source](https://ares-developers.github.io/Ares-docs/bugfixes/type3/pipsloading.html) | `code/display.cpp` | small | - |
